के राजपत्र , भाग 2, खंड 3, उपखंड ( ii ) तारीख 30 जनवरी, 1993 में प्रकाशित अधिमूचना सं . का . प्रा . 181 ( तारीख )

में प्रकाशित , भारत सरकार के कोयला मंत्रालय की अधिसूचना सं . का . पा . 90 तारीख 24 दिसम्बर , 1991 द्वार 
भूचना से मंलग्न अनुसूची में विनिर्दिष्ट परिक्षेत्र की भूमि में , जिसका माप 1831 . 40 एकड़ ( लग भग ) या 741 . 16 हैक्टर 
( लग भग ) है । संबंधी अधिकारों का अर्जन करने के अपने प्राशय की सूचना दी थी ; 

और सक्षम प्राधिकारी ने उक्त अधिनियम की धारा 8 के अनुसरण में केन्द्रीय सरकार को अपनी रिपोर्ट दे दी है ; 

और केन्द्रीय सरकार का पूर्वोक्त रिपोर्ट पर विचार करने के पश्चात और मध्य प्रदेश सरकार से परामर्श करने के 
पश्चात , यह समाधान हो गया है कि इससे संलग्न अनसूची में वर्णित 1806 . 70 एकड़ ( लगभग ) या 731 . 160 हैक्टर 
( लगभग ) माप वाली भूमि में खनन संबंधी अधिकार अजित किए जाने चाहिए । 

अत : केन्द्रीय सरकार, उक्त अधिनियम की धारा 9 को उपधारा ( 1 ) द्वारा प्रदत्त शक्तियों का प्रयोग करते हुए, यह 
घोपणा करती है कि इसमें संलग्न अनुसूची में वर्णित 1806 . 70 एकड़ ( लगभग ) या 731. 160 हैक्टर ( लग भग ) माप 
वाली भूमि में खनन संबंधी अधिकार अजित किए जाते हैं ।

का . प्रा . 1345 :...- केन्द्रीय सरकार ने कोयला धारक क्षेत्र ( अर्जन पार विकास ) अधिनियम , 1957 ( 1957 का 20 ) (जिसे इसमें इसके 
पश्चात उमत अधिनियम मह गया है ) की धारा 4 की उपधारा ( 1 ) के अधीन भारत के राजपत्र , भाग 2, खंड 3, उपखंड (ii ) , तारीख 22 जून , 1991 
में प्रकाशित भारत सरकार के तत्कालीन ऊर्जा मन्त्रालय , कोयला विभाग की अधिसूचना सं , का . मा . 1767 , तारीख 31 मई , 1991 द्वारा इस अधिसूचना 
से संलग्न अनुसूची में विनिर्दिष्ट परिक्षेत्र की 581 , 50 हैक्टर ( लगभग ) या 1436 . 88 एकड़ ( लगभग) भूमि में कोयणे का पूर्वेक्षण करने के अपने प्राशय 
की मूचना दी थी , 

और केन्द्रीय सरकार का यह समाधान हो गया है कि इम अधिसूचना में मंलग्न अनुसूची में वर्णित उक्म भूमि के भाग में कोयना अभिप्राप्त है । 

अनः अम्र केन्द्रीय सरकार , उक्त ममिनियम की धारा 7 की उधान ( 1 ) हारा प्रदत्त शक्किगों का प्रयोग करते हुए, इमसे संलग्न अनुमूची में वर्णित 
333 . 161 हैक्टर ( मगभग) या 823 . 21 एकड़ ( नगमग ) माप की भूमि में खनियों के खनन , नपान, बोर करने खुवाई करने और मलाश करने , उन्हें 
प्राप्त करने, उन पर कार्य करने पौर उन्हें ले जाने के अधिकारों का अर्जन करने के अपने प्राशय की सूचना देती है । 
टिप्पणी : -... इस अधिसूचना के अंतर्गत आने वाले क्षेत्र के रेखांक मं . एम . ई . सी . एल . बी . एम . पी . जी . एम . ( योजना ) भूमि/ 116, नारीख ३ मित्तम्बर , 

12, का निरीक्षण कलक्टर, शहडोल ( मध्य प्रदेश ) के कार्यालय में या कलकता के कार्यालय में या साउथ ईस्टर्न कोलफील्लम लिमिटेड गजम्ब

अनुभाग ) , मोपत क, बिमासपुर- 495001 ( मध्य प्रदेश ) के कार्यालय में पियाजाताहै । 
टिप्पणी : 2. उपस अधिनियम की धारा के सपबंधों की पोर यान माकृष्ट किया जाता है , जिसमें निम्नमिलित है : -- 


मम के प्रति मापत्तियो 


" १ . ( 1 ) काई पनि जो किसी भूमि में जिपको बास धार 1 के अयोन अधिसूपना निकाली गई , हितमय है , अधिसूचना के निकाले जाने से 
भीस दिन के मो . सम्पूर्ण भूमि या उसके किसी भाग या ऐसी भूमि में मा उग पर के किन्ही अधिकारों का अर्बन किए प्राने के बारे में प्रापत्ति कर सकेगा । 


राष्टीकरण ... इस धारा के अन्तिगर्त यह मापत्ति नही मानी जाएगी कि कोई व्यक्ति किसी भूमि में कोयला उत्पादन के लिए स्वयं बमन मभियाएं 
करनी चाहता है धीर ऐसी मभियाएं केन्द्रीय सरकार या किसी अन्य व्यक्ति को नहीं करनी चाहिए । 

( A ) धार ( 1) के अधीन प्रत्येक प्रापति सक्षम प्राधिकारी को लिखित रूप में की जाएगी और सम प्राधिकारी प्रापतिकर्ता को स्वयं सुने पाने 
फा या विधि थमायो नारा सुनवाई का अवसर बेमा मोर ऐसी समा प्रापत्तियों को सुनने के पश्चात और ऐसी अतिरिक्त जाप , यदि कोई हो , करने के 
पवार जो वह मावश्यक समझता है यह या तो धारा 7 को अधारा ( 1) के अधीन मधिसूचना भूमि के या ऐसी भूमि में या उस पर के अधिकारों के 
संमंध में एक रिपोर्ट या ऐसी ममि के विभिन्न दाहों या ऐसी भूमि में या उस पर के अधिकारों के संबंध में प्रापत्तियों पर अपनी सिफारिशों और उसके 
द्वारा की गई कार्यवाही के अभिलेन महिम विभिन्न रिपोर्ट केन्द्रीय सरकार को उसके विनिश्चय के लिए देगा । 


( 3 ) - धारा के प्रयोगन के लिए वह व्यति ।कसी भूमि में हिवस समा जाएगा जो प्रतिकार में हित का पता करने का हकदार होता 
अभिभूमि मा मो ममि में या म पर है अधिकार इस अधिनियम के अधीन प्रजित भार लिए आते हैं । "

581 . 50 hectares ( approximately ) or 1436 . 88 acres

the Schedule annexed to that notification ;

का . पा . 1349 . - --केन्द्रीय सरकार , सरकारी स्यान ( अप्राधिकृत अधिभोगियों की बेदखली ) अधिनियम , 1971, ( 1971 
का 40 ) की धारा 3 द्वारा प्रदत शक्तियों का प्रयोग करते हुए , नीचे की सारणी स्तंभ ( 1 ) में उल्लिखित पाई . बी . पी . 
कंपनी लिमिटेड के अधिकारियों को , जो सरकार के राजपत्रित अधिकारी की पंक्ति समतुल्प अधिकारी हैं , उक्त अधिनियम के 
प्रयोजनों के लिए सम्पदा अधिकारी नियुक्त करती है और उक्त अधिकारी उक्त सारणी के स्तम्भ ( 2 ) में विनिर्दिष्ट स्थानों की बाबत 
अपनी अधिकारिता की स्थानीय सीमाओं के भीतर , उक्त अधिनियम द्वारा या उसके अधीन मम्पदा अधिकारियों को प्रदत शक्तियों 
का प्रयोग और उन पर अधिरोपित कर्तव्यों का पालन करेंगे ।

लिमिटेड या उसके द्वारा या उसकी मोर से पट्टे पर लिए 
गए मा सन्निमण किए गए स्थान । 
पश्चिमी बंगाल, बिहार , उड़ीसा , असम और अन्य पूर्वोतर राज्यो में

AWARD 
This is a sefcrence mude to this Tribunal by the Labour 
Departinent, Government of India , vide letter No. L - 12012 / 
65791-IR ( E - II), for answering : 
"Whether the action of the Management of Bank of 

Maliarashiira Nagpur for rejecting the claim of Cyclo 
Aluwance with effect from 1 - 1 -1982 to Shri U , C . 
Kamble: (Sub- stat) is justified ? If not, to what 
relief is the workman entided 


Notice was issued on 29 - 7- 1991 to both the parties for 

Caring before this Tribunal on 27- 9 - 1991 . Nono appeared 
on behalt of the Union and a fresh notice was served and you 
no appearance was voado on behaif of the Union and the 
mutler was adjourned for ex parte hearing on 5 - 2 - 1992 , even 
on which date none on bezalf of the Union appeared . 


In the ab .cnce of any statement of claim it is not possible 
to know the nature of dispute . There was none who could 
be questioned on the point. It is therefore not possible to 
hold action of the management of Bapk of Mobarashtra as 
unjustificd , and the workman is therefore not etitled to any 
relief.

AWARD 
This is a reference made to this Tribunal vide letter No. 
L - 12012 / 464 / 90 - IR ( B - II ) dated 5th April 1991, by the 
Ministry of Labour, Government of India . 
The point to be answered is : 
" Whether the action of the Management of Bank of 

Maharashtra in imposing a punishment by stoppage 
of six annual increments on Shri K . D . Sonar 
Musalkar is justifica If not, what relief the work 
man is entitled to ? 


PRESENT : 
Sri C . N . Sasidharan . Industrial Tribunal, 

JN 
Industrial Dispute No. 21 /92 

BETWEEN 
The Managing Director, State Bank of Travancore, Hoad 

Office Poojappura , Trivandrum . 


On receipt of this reference , notices were issued to both 
the partios for appearing before this Tribunal on 14 - 5 - 1991 . 
But no onc appeared on behalf of the workman . 


AND 
The General Secretary , State Bank of Travancore Staff 

Union , Central Office, P .B . No. 5601, Trivandrum , 


The Management, namely the Bank of Maharashtra in its 
statement dated 28- 8 - 1992 , it has been contended that the 
workman has boon penalised for having defrauded the Bank 
by withdrawing a sum of Rs. 500 by forging the signature 
of an account holder and he also committed other misconduct 
as detailed in the chargcshoct. The Bank further stated that 
the workman had admitted the fact in the departmental 
enquiry conducted against him . It was further stated that 
no statement has been filed on behalf of the workman 
inspitc of several opportunities given to him , 


AWARD 
This industrial dispute has been referred for adjudication 
to this Tribunal by the Government of India as per order 
No. L - 12012 / 57 / 92-IR ( B -111) dated 20 - 7 - 1992 for adjudicat 
ing the following issuo ; 
“ Whether the action on tho part of the management 

of State Bank of Tranvancoro in denying the post 
of DCCP to Sri Mohankumar at the Peyad Branch 
of the bank is fair and justifiablo ? If not , to what 
relief the said workman is entitled to ? " 


It is seen from the Rojnama, maintained in this office, 
that the workman was represented by Mr. C . N , Kulkarni on 
26- 6 - 1991 after receipt of the notice . The matter canie to 
bo adjourned for filing of the statoment of claim by the 
Union , but no statement came to be filed on the following 
datcs also . Instead of filing the statement of claim adjourn 
ment was sought, On 16 - 1 - 1992 none appeared on behalf 
of both the parties, and notico was issued to the Union for 
filling the statement of claim on 13 - 4 - 1992 . Thereafter , 
notice for cx - parte hearing on 24 -6 - 1992 , and thereafter, 
on 9th July 1992 . The matter came to be adjourned further 
as the Presiding Officer of this Tribunal was not appointed . 
It was then fixed on 27th April 1993 , and no appearance was 
mado on behalf of either of the parties. Under this circuns 
tances , in the absence of any material to show that the 
Action of the managemet of Bank of Maharashtra in imposing 
ine punishment of stoppage of six annual increment as unjust, 
that it is not 50 . 


2 . The union representing the workmap Sri Mohan Kumar 
has filed a cluim statement and the contentions are briefly 
og below : The subordinate staff in the management Bank are 
cntitled to special allowance for discharying certain specified 
duties as per the terms of servicu applicable to them . The 
post of Daftary - Cum - Cash Poon ( DCCP for whort ) is ono 
uitracting Special allowance . Every brauch of this Bank 
hay DCCP . " The post of DCCP al Peyad branch fell vacant on 
7 -8 - 1989. The two peong working in that branch on that day 
weid on probation . Temporary employees and employees on 
probation are not be entrusted with any duties attracting 
Special allowance is per staff circular of the Bank 
datad 7 - 3 - 1992 . The workman Sri Mohan Kumar was con 
firmed in the Bank s service with affect from 20 - 1- 1990 and he 
has been doing the duties of DCCP from the date . But tho 
management did not pay him special allowance and the refugal 
of the Danagement to confum him as DCCP with effoct from 
20 - 1 - 1990 is justifiable and fllcgal. Sri Mohan Kumar was 
initially posted to the Zonal Office of the Bank on 20 - 7 - 1989 
along with five others. He was finally posted to Peyad branch 
on 27 - 10 - 1989 . Sri K . Surendran posted as peon joined at 
Peyad branch on 25- 10 - 1989 . The post of DCCP of that 
branclı fell vacant on 7 - 8 - 1989. Neither of the poons of 
that timo was posted as DCCP and not paid special allowance 
a s both of them were on probation . The Regional Manager

It is specifically stated in Ext. W - 2 that on the basis of 
circular dated 7- 3 - 1972 of the Bank DCCP allowance is 
payable only after confirmation and as such Sri Mohan Kumar 
is eligible for the post of DCCP. Ext. W - 4 is a letter 
addrcysed to the manager of Punnayurkulam branch from the 
Regional Manager. It is stated in Ext. W - 4 that as per staff 
circular dated 16 - 3 - 1991 tomporary cinployees and employees 
on probation should not be entrusted with any of the duties 
attracting special allowance. As per Ext. W -4 the Regional 
Manager has pointed that payment of DCCP allowance to an 
employee on probation in that branch was not in order . 
It was also pointed out that another confirmed employce 
in the branch alone is eligible for DCCP allowance , Ext, W - 1 
W - 2 and W - 4 make it clear that the practice followed in the 
Bank for the posts eligible for DCCP allowance were given 
only after confirmation . The management has not furnished 
any circular to show that thc employce on probation was also 
given the post of DCCP . No specific instances of such 
posting were ulso brought to the notice of this Tribunal 
to support the contention of management in this regard . 
It is also not established by producing any circular or 
pointing out any specific instances that the normal practice 
followed for filling up of special allowance carrying posts 
for subordinate staff is centrewise branch seniosity . It is 
also not established that pepiority is normally counted 
with reference to the date of joining at the branch and not 
on the date of confirmation . On the other hand Exts. W - 1 and 
W - 2 clearly establish that only confirmed cmployees as peon 
have been given posting in special allowance carrying post. 
It is true that Sri Surendran joined the Peyad branch on 
25- 10 - 1989 and Sri Mohan Kumar joined there on 27 - 10 1989 
only . But as I have held above only confirmed employees are 
eligible for tho post attracting special allowance . So the 
action of management in denying the post of DCCP to Sri 
Mohan Kumar is unfair and unjustifiable. The management 
has thus overlooked the claim of Sri Mohan Kumar and given 
the posting to Sri K , Surendran which to highly illegal and 
unjust. On the basis of Exts. W - 1 , W - 2 and W - 4 and on the 
basis of precedents followed in tho Bank Sri Mohan Kumar 
alone is eligible for DCCP allowance in Peyad branch with 
effect from 20 - 1 , 1990 . 


on representation from Sri Mohan Kumar adviced the branch 
Manager that Sri Mohan Kumar is eligible for the post of 
DCCP from the date of confirmation . Sri Mohan Kumar was 
the only confirmed pcon at Peyad brunch as on 20 - 1 - 1990 who 
wus entitled to the post of DCCP. The management confirmed 
Sri Surendran algo on 30 - 8 - 1990 and it was communicated 
only on February 1992 i. c. during the pendency of this dis 
pute . It is therefore illegal and Liablo to be oullified . The 
management in a similar case directed to pay special allow 
ance to an employee who was transferred to Punnayurkulam 
brunch of the Bunk -with effect from the date ho joined there 
tbough thero was already another pequ who onjoyed branch 
benjority , but was on probation . The denial of the post of 
DCCP to Sri Mohan Kumar amounts to discrimination . The 
action of managaoment in designating Sri Surendran as DCCP 
and payment of special allowance with effect from 10 - 2 - 1992 
during the pendency of this disputo is it violation of S : c . 33 
of the Industrial Disputes Act. The prayer is for confirma 
tion of Sri Mohan Kumar as DCCP of Peyad branch with 
effect from 20 - 1- 1990 continuously and for paynient of 
special allowanco . 

3 . The management opposed the claim of the union agul 
the contentions of management are briefly as under : 
Sri Damodaran who was working as peon in Poyad branch 

way receiving DCCP allowance til 25- 11- 1989 on 
which date he was transferred toom there , Sri K . 
Şurendran newly recruited as peon joined Peyad 
branch on 25- 10 - 1989. Sri Mohan Kumar newly 
recruited as peon joined Trivandrum Zonal Office of 
the Bank on 20 - 7 . 1989 and ho joined Peynd branch 
ou 27 - 10- 1989. Sri Mohan Kumar was . confirmed 
with effect from 20 - 1 - 1990 as peon and Sri Surendran 
was confirmed with effect from 25 -4 . 1990 . So the 
claim of the union based on letter dated 30 - 8 -1990 
stated 10 be of the Regional Manager was not given 
effect to and the management was only considering 
the cligibility of Sri Surendran Also . to get the 
allowance . The decision to designate $ ri Surendran 
as DCCP was taken on 19 - 10 - 1991 which was com 
municated to the Deputy General Mapsger 01 
13 - 1 - 1992 . The letter of the Asst. Labour Com 
missioner intimating his decision to discuss the 
dispute raised by the union was received : on 17 - 1 - 1992 
and there is no violation of any practice of law . 
The normal practice followed in the Bank for filling 
up special allowance carrying posts is centerwino / 
branch seniority . Senlority is normally counted with 
reference to tho date of joining the branch and not 
based on the date of confirmation . Confirmation of 
service alono is not the only criterion for deciding, 
eligibility . In Punnayurkulam branch the two peonts 
joined on the same day and scriority was counted 
based on tho date of birth of the concerned cmp 
loycos . This has no connection with the present 
case . The service conclition of Sri Mohan Kumar 
was " not altered during the pendency of conciliation 
proceedings. Sri Mohan Kumor is not entitled to 
the post of DXCCP in Peyad branch as he was not 
senior in that branch . According to the manage 

ment the workman is not entitled to any relief . 
4 . No evidence has been adduced by ciber side: How 
ever the union has produced six documents and the manage 
ment has produced one document. I am markings six docu 
ment, as Ext. W - 1 to W -6 on the side of the Union and Ext . 
M - 1 on the side of the management as there was no objection 
from either side for marking theretail documents in evidence . 

5. The claion of the unlon is for designating Sri Mohafi 
Kumar as DCCP of Peya i branch with effect from 20 - 1- 1990 , 
the date on which he was confirmed in the Bank s service 
and for payment of allowance continuously from that day 
onwards, Admittetlly Sri Mohan Kumar was confirmed in the 
Bank s service with effect fronı 20 - 1- 1990 95 peon and 
Sri Surendran was confirmed with effect from 25- 4 - 1990 
as peon . The management designated Sri Surendran as DCCP 
on the basis of his branch seniority . According to the unior 
temporary employees and employces on probation should 
not he entrusted with any of the cluties attracting Special 
allowance . Reliance was placed on Exts . W - 1 staff circular 
issued by the Deritty Genernt Manager of the Management 
wherein it is specifically stated 90 . Ext. W - 2 la copy of 
tetter from the Roplopal Manager of the Bank to the . Peyad 
branch regarding DCCP allowance to subordinato staff . 


6 . In the result, an award is passed holding that the action 
on the part of the management of State Bank of Travancoro 
in deying the post of DCCP to Sri Mohan Kumar at the Peyad 
branch is unfair and unjustifiable. Sri Mohan Kumar 
accordingly entitled to get the posting as DCCP of Peyad 
branch continuously with effect from 20 - 1 - 1990 and also 
special allowance for the entire period .

shifts in the factual position in a chronological order stated 
hercunder can help easier understanding of the matter in dis 
pute . 


FIRST STAGE : Before the merger of the minor port of 
Tuticoria and New Port of Tuticorin which took place on 
Ist ripril, 1979 , the Shipping Ministry of the Ceutral Govern 
ment was in direct control in the absence of a major port 
trust. Prior to 1st April, 1979 the ratio 3 : 1 was observed 
by the Central Government in filling the posty of U . D . Cs by 
promoting L . D . Cs. 


SECOND STAGE : Now the merger of the two ports into 
Tuticorin New Port coming ucder the purvie w of Major Port 
Truis vlct has taken place with a Board of Trustees taking 
over the reins of the major rorts aministration , The Board 
of Trustecs is empowered u / s 28 of the Major Port Trusts 
Aci to muhe regulations of rules in respect of appointment, 
promotion etc . S . 124 of thç sume het enacts that any such 
regulations or rules can have effect only after they get approv 
ed by tho Shipping Ministry of thc Central Government and 
thc said upproval gets published in thc Gazette . While so , the 
board changed the pre 1st April, 1979 rule giving 3 : 1 ratio 
into ) ; 1 which has the effect of reducing the quotit of 
promoices on the basis of seniority , in the grade of L . D . Cs. 
This new rule offective from 1st April, 1979 is one approved 
by the Central Government.

Act, 1963. In any view of the matter it must be deemded to 
te a valid settlement in as much as it has been rightly inple 
Ilenied by the Chairman between 1979 and 1986 . The clore , 
it is not open to the Chairman of the Port Irtist to deviato

in according approval by the Central Government pod chunge

any direction from the board of [ rustees cannot review the 
Icgality of 3 ; 1 ratio , brand the promotion on that basis 
as ad hoc and make a volte - face . It is interesting to note 
that the Central Government has finally approved on 29th 
January , 1987 vide Ex. M - 11 notification, the settlenient as 
embodied in the draft amendment to the regulation pres 
cubing 3 . 1 for promotion of 1, , D . Cs. It is therefore clear 
that the Chairman s order / orders under which he has given 
retrospective promotion including some prospective prono 
tions, it any to the 1 . D . Cs on the basis of merit against 
50 per cent vacancies and thereby divested the seniority 
based fromotees , of their promotion and ranks in the grade 
of U . D . Cs are illegal. As a result, it is but necessary 10 
cancel all the promotions given by the Chairman on the 
hasis of merit against 50 per cent vacancies , namely , 1 : 1 
ratio and protect the interests of U . D . Cs alrcady promot 
ed as such against 75 per cent quota on the basis of genio 
rity of 1. D . Cs. It follows that revised promotion or re 
vised seniority ordered by the Chairman in terms of Ex. 
W - 7 und 1: x . M - 15 giving more posts to the merit quota is 
contrary to the settlerhent Ex. W - 11 which is true, valid 
and lawful and which cannot be wished away by the 109 
pondent or even by the Central Government. 


STAGE III : However the new approved rule prescribing 
1 : 1 ratio to becomo operative from 1st April, 1979 was not 
allowed to be implemented by the workman ( L . D . Ç . ) vche 
mently opposed to the ratio . This resultcu in negotiations lead 
ing to the settlement u / s , 12 ( 3 ) Ex. W - 8 and another settle 
ment couched in the form of conclusions reached in the 
course of discussion , us recorded in the minutos Ex, W -11. We 
have already seen that the board of Trustees has ucccricut 
Ex, W - 11 3grcement as true ant gave their approval thcreto 
and the Chairman wrote to the Central Government seeking 
the Government s approval u / s. 124 of the Major Port Trust 
Act, vide Ex . M - 10 dated 10th March , 1981. 


STAGE IV : Jo view of the agreement Ex, W - 11 the 
Board s approval and the anticipated approval to be given by 
the Central Government the Chairman filled up the post of 
U . D . C . by adopting 3 : 1 ratio and hereby keeping the 
approvet rule providing 1 : 1 ration in abeyence . Thus, 1 ; 1 
ratio even though ordained by the approve ,i rule was given 
a go -by even in 1979 because the management of the New 
Port Trust has accepted 3 : 1 ratio for all practical purposes 
and filled up the vacant posts of U . D . Cs by glving 75 per 
cent of the posts to the seniors in the grade of tho L . D . Cs. 
This implemontation of 3 : 1 ratio by ignoring 1 : 1 ralio , 
has been made continuously upto May , 1986 . 


STAGE V and LAST STAGE , : The anticipated - pproval 
hy the Central Governmont, that is , Shipping Ministry has tot 
hecn given betwecn 1980 and 1986 . Uusing this delay, which is 
inordinale, the Chairman of the Port Trust has deviated from 
the agreed and accepted ratio of 3 : 1 and suddenly honoured 
the approved rulc of 1979 which provides 1 : 1 ratio , on the 
pled that the Chairman cannot flout an approved fute any 
longer and the Mcrit quotu of 50 per cent ( 1 : 1) should be 
filled up retrospectively from 1979. This stand of the Chair 
man is reflected in his policy note file Ex. M - 15 . 


( 10 ) After giving promotions on the basis of accepted 
3 : 1 ratio between 19 - 12 - 80 and 9 - 4 - 86 , the respondent or 
thc Chairman thereof cannot turn the tables arbitrarily. In 
any view , in action on the part of the Central Government 
till September , 1987 ( the date of Ex. M - 11) does not nican 
negation of Ex , W - 11 settlement, or draft amendment to 
the rule , by the Central Government. We cannot read in 
Section 124 of the Major Port Trust Act any power con 
ferred on the Central Government to over ride a settlement 
such as Ex . W - 11 which has all the attributes of Section 
1213 ) settlement. After considering the facts appearing in 
the evidence and the position of law , I find on this point 
that all actions of the Respondent -Chairman relating to pro . 
motion of lower division clerks as upper division clerks on 
the basis of the 1 : 1 ratio , rcversion of already promoted 
candidates and preparation of revised seniority list of U . D . Cs 
on the basis of 1 : 1 ratio are illegal and liable to be set 
aside . The Respondent hus vainly characterised the reference 
or rather the form of the qucstion referred to as factually 
incorrect or meaningless . The question referrod to is no 
doubt improperly worded but its ineaning is apparent. Tlie 
question fortuins only to the actions of tlic Chairman as 
reficc !cd in his circular dated 10 - 4 - 1986 . The point which 

I have formulated for determination reflects the correct 
meaning of the reference . 


( 9 ) A major lacuna found by the respondent in the agree 
ment or settlement contained in Ex , W - 11 is that it fails to 
meet the incredients of a settlement as defined in the J. D . 
Act. The agreement I believe is a direct and logical off shoot 
of the carlier Sec , 12 ( 3 ) se ltlement Ex . W - 8 . A few other 
demands have been actually discussed in the presçnce of the 
Conciliation Oficer as shown by Ex , M -4 . The agreement on 
3 : 1 ratio is the only one item not directly arrived at in the

conciliation officer in very much implied in the entire course

actions taken by the Respondent and its Chairman with 
regard to promotion of lower division clarks by adhering to 
1 : 1 ratio on the basis of Ex . W - 7 circular dated 10 - 4 - 1986 
are invalid and that all the promotions effected on the batis

of 3 : 1 ratio from 1 -4 - 1979 onwards shall remain valid and

2 . All the threc references named above were hear 
analogous and are disposed o1! by a common award , 
It was the comnion case of all the three concerned 
workmen that they were under the employment of the 
management of Indian Airlines as casual labour. They 
worked for several years completing more than 24 ) 
days in a calendar year. But suddenly they were stop 
ped without any notice or notice pay. They were 
appointed orally with the assurance that appointment 
letters will be given in due course but lastly no such 
letters were issued . The nianagement issued tempora . y 
permit in proof of employment but did not regularisc 
the services inspite of the firct that the concerned 
workmen rendered the services for years together. The 
management while terminating the services of the 
concerned workmen did not follow the procedure laid 
down under Section 256 of the 1. D . Act and the 
services of the juniur hands namely Jagdevan Singh , 
Rabindra Mandal , Rajendra Kumar and others were 
retained. The management also ſailed to follow the rule 
77 of the Industrial Disputes ( Central) Rules , 1957 
under which it was obligatory on the part of the 
nianagement to maintain seniority list of the workmen 
in respect of category and to display the same on the 
notice board for employment of retrenched workmen . 
Thc stoppage of the work of the concerned workmen 
is retrenchment which is illegal, bad in law for the 
management failcd to issue one months notice, notice 
pay and retrenchment compensation which are con 
dition precedent to be followed urder the provision 
of Section 25F of the 1. D . Act, Tliu workmen are 
thus entitled for their reinstatement with full back 
wages.

The Government of India , Ministry of Labour in 
exercise of the powers conferred on them under Section 
10 ( 1 ) ( d ) of the 1. D . Act, 19 .17 has referred the 
following disputes to this Tribunal for adjudication 
vide their order s referred to above.

of Shri Mahesh Prasad Gupta , ex -Casual 


3 . The management filed W .S . seperately in all three 
references and the common grounds taken are as 
follows : 

ſt way stated that the alleged dispute is not an 
industriul dispute within the uçaring of Section 2 ( k )

of the 1. D . Act . The casual workmen got work tein 
porarily in absence of the regular employces and they 
do not have any guaranteed right of employment. The

The employers corporation operates flight to and 
from Paina Airport but it is not possible to adhere 
strict time schedule for its operation depends upon so

Reference No. 17190 
Shri Chandrika Prasad is the concerned workman 
who stated to have joined the services in Indian Air 
lincs at Patna , Eastern Region in the year 1980 as

nient for more than 8 years in continuous service. 


Loading off loading and cleaning of aircraft is the 
jub of regular nature but it looses its character as 
such due to aforesaid various compelling circumstances . 
The corporation has got perinancnt manpower who 
are in the roll for the purpose of loading off loading 
and also for cleaning aircraft but it has to be engaged 
part time daily rated casuals hadlies for the reasons 
stated above and also on account of leave replacement . 
Such engagements are made for specified period which 
is also within the knowledge of the persons so engaged . 
Thus the concerned workmen were engaged intermit 
tently depending upon the requirement of the work 
which were of specific nature and for specific period . 
Therefore the concerned workmen cannot claim em 
ployinent as a matter of right employment. It was also 
stated that no concened workman ever completed 240 . 
days attendance in any preceding 12. calendar months. 


The workmen alleged that he was paid less through 
cash voucher although he worked for full time. The 
delails of which find mention in para - 13 of the W . S .

Patna but it was of no use. Lastly an industrial dispute 
was raisid which ended in failure giving rise to the 
present referencc . 


It was stated that the employer corporation is a 
statutory corporation established under Section 3 of 
the Air Corporation Act, 1953 (being Act No . 27 of 
1953 ) . Under Section 7 of the Act the principal 
function of the corporation has been noted which 
amongst others is to provide safe, « llicient, adequate, 
economically and properly coordinated air transport 
services to the best advantage of the people . Again 
Section 45 ( clause -2 ) (b ) provides ihat the employers 
corporation may make regulation relating to the terms 
and condition of the officers and other employees of 
the corporation .

And 1989.

for appointment.
